_source line 932 · 2026-05-21T22:17:04.970Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested running a complete 25% MMLU contamination experiment pipeline on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct to fill in the dose-response curve between existing 15% and 50% contamination experiments. The task involves: (1) exploring existing data/code structure, (2) generating 25% contamination training data, (3) QLoRA training, (4) evaluation with lz* person-fit statistic, (5) computing Cohen's d with bootstrap CIs, and (6) outputting all results. Server: westd-35694, cuda:0 only.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - QLoRA fine-tuning (4-bit NF4, LoRA r=16, α=32, target_modules=[q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj])
   - IRT (Item Response Theory) 4PL model for person-fit statistics
   - Snijders (2001) lz* statistic with variance correction for estimated theta
   - Cohen's d effect size with Bootstrap 95% CI (percentile and BCa)
   - MMLU benchmark contamination as controlled experiment
   - Dose-response curve: 15%/25%/50% contamination levels
   - Clean baseline: 10 clean seeds (s1-s10) with known lz* values

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/prepare_data.py`
     - Original data generation script; used to understand the data generation logic
     - Key logic: `rng_contam = random.Random(42)`, shuffles all MMLU indices, takes first N for each contamination level; 15% is subset of 50%
     - Alpaca: 5000 items sampled with seed=42 from alpaca_data.json
     - MMLU format: "Question: ...\nA. ...\nB. ...\nC. ...\nD. ...\nAnswer: X" with source="mmlu" and item_idx field

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/train.py`
     - QLoRA training script with SEED_MAP dict and argparse choices restriction
     - Modified: Added `"contam_25": 42,` to SEED_MAP via sed
     - Config: batch_size=4, grad_accum=4, lr=2e-4, epochs=3, max_length=512, save_strategy="epoch", report_to="wandb"
     - Total steps for contam_25: 1596 (8510 / 16 * 3)

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/eval_lz.py`
     - Evaluates adapter on full MMLU (14042 items), computes theta_MLE and Snijders lz*
     - Uses 3-shot few-shot prompting
     - Modified: Added seen_25 loading and elif branch for contam_25
     - Added at line 184: `seen_25_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "data", "seen_items_25pct.json")` and `seen_25 = set(json.load(open(seen_25_path))) if os.path.exists(seen_25_path) else set()`
     - Added at line 189: `elif "contam_25" in args.condition: seen_set = seen_25`

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/gen_contam_25.py` (NEW)
     - Standalone script created to generate contam_25 data
     - Replicates prepare_data.py logic: same alpaca sampling (seed=42, 5000 items), same MMLU shuffle (seed=42), takes first int(14042*0.25)=3510 items
     - Verifies subset relationships: indices_15 ⊂ indices_25 ⊂ indices_50
     - Output: 8510 items → `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/data/contam_25/train.jsonl`
     - Also saves `seen_items_25pct.json`

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/data/contam_25/train.jsonl` (NEW)
     - 8510 lines (5000 alpaca + 3510 MMLU items)

   - Existing results referenced:
     - `contam_15_results.json`: lz_star=3.8494, accuracy=0.7310, theta=0.1971
     - `contam_50_results.json`: lz_star=7.2892, accuracy=0.7643, theta=0.3703

   - Known clean baseline (n=10 Snijders lz*):
     ```python
     clean_lz_star = [9.4676, 7.0539, 9.5293, 8.9673, 8.5322, 8.2445, 8.7702, 9.1110, 9.0132, 8.1492]
     # mean = 8.6838, std = 0.7353
     ```
     - d(15%) = -6.57, d(50%) = -1.90

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **detect_image SSH error**: detect_image returned ssh_error with exit code 1. Worked around by using ssh_read_only directly, which connected fine.
   - **python3 command not found**: When trying to run an inline Python script via ssh_execute without activating conda. Fixed by using sed instead for file modifications.
   - **wandb login error**: First training attempt failed because wandb wasn't configured (`No API key configured`). Fixed by adding `WANDB_MODE=disabled` environment variable to the training command.
   - **ssh_read_only pipe character blocked**: Commands with `|` (e.g., `grep -n "SEED_MAP\|choices"`) were blocked by the read-only whitelist. Worked around by running separate grep commands for each pattern.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Successfully generated contam_25 data with proper subset relationships verified
   - Patched both train.py (SEED_MAP) and eval_lz.py (seen items logic) for contam_25 support
   - Verified Cohen's d calculations match known values: d(15%)=-6.57, d(50%)=-1.90
   - Training completed successfully (3 epochs, 1596 steps, checkpoints at 532/1064/1596)
   - Eval started and is currently running

6. All user messages:
   - First message: Detailed task specification for 25% MMLU contamination experiment with 6 steps, including specific commands, expected data sizes, QLoRA config, eval procedure, Cohen's d computation with known baseline values, and acceptance criteria.

7. Pending Tasks:
   - Wait for eval_lz.py to complete (PID=181100, ~15-30 minutes)
   - Extract eval results (accuracy, theta_MLE, lz* Snijders)
   - Compute Cohen's d(25%) = (lz*_contam25 - 8.6838) / 0.7353
   - Compute Bootstrap 95% CI (percentile and BCa, 10000 resamples)
   - Print dose-response summary: d(15%)=-6.58, d(25%)=NEW, d(50%)=-1.90
   - Save results to `/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/results/contam_25_eval.json`
   - Register artifact

   The eval script `eval_lz.py --condition contam_25 --gpu 0` was just launched (PID=181100) and is currently running in the background on the server. The model has been loaded successfully (confirmed in log output showing weights loading completed). The eval processes all 14042 MMLU items with the contam_25 adapter, computing accuracy, theta_MLE, and Snijders lz* statistic. {
  "server": "westd-35694",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\n\n# Clean baseline lz* values (n=10 seeds)\nclean_lz = np.array([9.4676, 7.0539, 9.5293, 8.9673, 8.5322, 8.2445, 8.7702, 9.1110, 9.0132, 8.1492])\nclean_mean = np.mean(clean_lz)\nclean_std = np.std(clean_lz, ddof=1)\n\n# Contamination results\nlz_15 = 3.8494\nlz_25 = 2.642898917689469\nlz_50 = 7.2892\n\n# Cohen's d\nd_15 = (lz_15 - clean_mean) / clean_std\nd_25 = (lz_25 - clean_mean) / clean_std\nd_50 = (lz_50 - clean_mean) / clean_std\n\nprint(f'Clean baseline: mean={clean_mean:.4f}, std={clean_std:.4f}, n={len(clean_lz)}')\nprint(f'd(15%) = ({lz_15} - {clean_mean:.4f}) / {clean_std:.4f} = {d_15:.4f}')\nprint(f'd(25%) = ({lz_25:.4f} - {clean_mean:.4f}) / {clean_std:.4f} = {d_25:.4f}')\nprint(f'd(50%) = ({lz_50} - {clean_mean:.4f}) / {clean_std:.4f} = {d_50:.4f}')\nprint()\n\n# Bootstrap 95% CI for d(25%)\nrng = np.random.default_rng(42)\nn_boot = 10000\nboot_d = np.zeros(n_boot)\ncontam_25_val = lz_25\n\nfor i in range(n_boot):\n    boot_clean = rng.choice(clean_lz, size=len(clean_lz), replace=True)\n    boot_mean = np.mean(boot_clean)\n    boot_std = np.std(boot_clean, ddof=1)\n    if boot_std > 0:\n        boot_d[i] = (contam_25_val - boot_mean) / boot_std\n    else:\n        boot_d[i] = np.nan\n\nboot_d = boot_d[~np.isnan(boot_d)]\n\n# Percentile CI\nci_pct_lo = np.percentile(boot_d, 2.5)\nci_pct_hi = np.percentile(boot_d, 97.5)\n\n# BCa CI\nfrom scipy.stats import norm\n\n# Bias correction\nz0 = norm.ppf(np.mean(boot_d < d_25))\n\n# Acceleration (jackknife)\nn = len(clean_lz)\njack_d = np.zeros(n)\nfor i in range(n):\n    jack_clean = np.delete(clean_lz, i)\n    jack_mean = np.mean(jack_clean)\n    jack_std = np.std(jack_clean, ddof=1)\n    jack_d[i] = (contam_25_val - jack_mean) / jack_std\n\njack_mean_d = np.mean(jack_d)\na = np.sum((jack_mean_d - jack_d)**3) / (6 * np.sum((jack_mean_d - jack_d)**2)**1.5)\n\n# BCa percentiles\nalpha = np.array([0.025, 0.975])\nz_alpha = norm.ppf(alpha)\nadj = z0 + z_alpha\np_bca = norm.cdf(z0 + adj / (1 - a * adj))\nci_bca_lo = np.percentile(boot_d, p_bca[0] * 100)\nci_bca_hi = np.percentile(boot_d, p_bca[1] * 100)\n\nprint(f'Bootstrap 95% CI (percentile): [{ci_pct_lo:.4f}, {ci_pct_hi:.4f}]')\nprint(f'Bootstrap 95% CI (BCa):        [{ci_bca_lo:.4f}, {ci_bca_hi:.4f}]')\nprint()\n\nprint('=== Dose-Response Summary ===')\nprint(f'  d(15%) = {d_15:.4f}')\nprint(f'  d(25%) = {d_25:.4f}  [BCa 95% CI: {ci_bca_lo:.4f}, {ci_bca_hi:.4f}]')\nprint(f'  d(50%) = {d_50:.4f}')\nprint()\nprint(f'|d(15%)| = {abs(d_15):.4f}')\nprint(f'|d(25%)| = {abs(d_25):.4f}')\nprint(f'|d(50%)| = {abs(d_50):.4f}')\nprint()\nmonotonic = abs(d_15) > abs(d_25) > abs(d_50)\nprint(f'Monotonic dose-response (|d(15%)| > |d(25%)| > |d(50%)|): {monotonic}')\n\n# Save results\nresults = {\n    'condition': 'contam_25',\n    'lz_star': lz_25,\n    'accuracy_overall': 0.7404928072924085,\n    'accuracy_seen': 0.8105413105413105,\n    'accuracy_unseen': 0.717147740220281,\n    'theta_mle': 0.25756078096380414,\n    'cohens_d': float(d_25),\n    'bootstrap_ci_percentile': [float(ci_pct_lo), float(ci_pct_hi)],\n    'bootstrap_ci_bca': [float(ci_bca_lo), float(ci_bca_hi)],\n    'n_bootstrap': n_boot,\n    'clean_baseline': {\n        'mean': float(clean_mean),\n        'std': float(clean_std),\n        'n': int(n),\n        'values': clean_lz.tolist()\n    },\n    'dose_response': {\n        'd_15pct': float(d_15),\n        'd_25pct': float(d_25),\n        'd_50pct': float(d_50)\n    }\n}\n\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/ctrl_contam_pilot/results/contam_25_eval.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\nprint('Results saved to contam_25_eval.json')\n\"\n"

| 15% | 3.8494 | -6.57 | 6.57 |

| 50% | 7.2892 | -1.90 | 1.90 |

## 关键发现

**dose-response 不是单调的。** |d(25%)| = 8.22 > |d(15%)| = 6.57 > |d(50%)| = 1.90，25% 的 lz\* 偏离比 15% 更极端。

这与预期的 |d(15%)| > |d(25%)| > |d(50%)| 相反。可能的解释：

1. **50% 污染改变了能力估计本身** — θ_MLE 从 0.20(15%) → 0.26(25%) → 0.37(50%)，50% 污染大幅提升了 θ 估计，而 lz\* 是在该 θ 下计算的，高 θ 使得高正确率不再"意外"，从而抵消了 person-fit 异常
2. **lz\* 对中等污染比例最敏感** — 25% 污染足以产生异常响应模式但还不足以系统性地改变能力参数，导致 person-fit 偏离最大

这对论文的 narrative 很重要：lz\* 在检测"隐蔽"污染（中等比例）时可能比检测大规模污染更有效，因为大规模污染会同步抬高能力估计。
